EXPERT ADVICE: - Absolute priority: check and handle duplicates/leakage
  - Compute exact hashes (e.g., md5/SHA1 on raw bytes) to detect identical images and perceptual hashes (phash/dhash) for near-duplicates across train and test.
  - Use these hashes to:
    - Build a lookup map for test-time: if a test image matches a train hash, assign the train label directly.
    - Group duplicates in CV: use StratifiedGroupKFold (groups = exact hash or a cluster id of near-duplicates) to prevent inflated OOF from same-image leakage across folds.
  - If many test images are duplicates, the lookup+fallback model route reliably yields 1.000 AUC.

- Backbone/strategy for 32x32
  - Best bet: ResNet18 pretrained with inputs upscaled to 64–96 px. If staying at 32 px, modify stem (3×3, stride 1, remove maxpool).
  - Alternatives: custom small CNN (3–5 conv blocks) at 32 px can hit 1.0; EfficientNet-B0 at 96 px is fine but unnecessary; B3 is overkill.
  - Start with ResNet18@96. Switch to custom CNN only if OOF <0.999 after fixes.

- Validation
  - Use 5-fold StratifiedGroupKFold (shuffle=True, seed fixed). Groups = exact hash (preferred) or near-duplicate cluster id. If grouping is hard, at minimum deduplicate exact copies before splitting.
  - Track per-fold and overall OOF AUC; aim for very low fold variance (<0.001).

- Augmentations that help vs hurt
  - Use light, geometry-focused augs: HorizontalFlip, VerticalFlip, RandomRotate90 or small rotations (±10–15°), small Shift/Scale (≤10%).
  - Optional: light ColorJitter (±0.1–0.2 brightness/contrast) and small Cutout (1 hole, ~8×8).
  - Avoid heavy color changes, large rotations, Mixup/CutMix (often hurts on tiny, easy images).

- Training specifics
  - Resize: 64–96 for pretrained (bicubic). Keep 32 for custom CNNs.
  - Normalization: ImageNet stats for pretrained; dataset stats for custom CNN (or BN with [0,1] inputs).
  - Optimizer/Loss/Schedule: AdamW + BCEWithLogitsLoss; OneCycleLR or cosine with warmup.
  - LRs: head ~1e-3, backbone ~1e-4; warmup 1–2 epochs on head, then unfreeze all, or unfreeze from start with lower backbone LR—both work here.
  - Batch size: 128–256 (tiny images). Epochs: 8–20 with early stopping (patience 2–3).

- Inference
  - Implement hybrid: first use the train-hash lookup for exact matches; otherwise use model prediction.
  - TTA: average original + hflip (optionally vflip). Skip multi-crop; little gain.

- Expected OOF/LB and ensembling
  - With duplicate handling and grouped CV, a single ResNet18@96 often hits 0.999–1.000 OOF and 1.000 LB.
  - If you’re stuck at ~0.999, average 2–3 seeds or 2 light backbones (ResNet18 + EffNet-B0).

- Class imbalance and metric notes
  - Mild imbalance (~3:1). Stratified CV is enough; no pos_weight/oversampling needed.
  - ROC-AUC is threshold-agnostic; submit probabilities. Don’t tune thresholds for this comp.

Concrete baseline to implement next
1) Preprocess
   - Compute exact (SHA1) and perceptual hashes for all images.
   - Build groups by exact hash; optionally merge near-duplicate hashes into same group.
2) CV
   - StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=5, random_state=42, shuffle=True).
3) Model
   - ResNet18 pretrained; input 96×96; ImageNet normalization; replace head with 1-unit BCE.
4) Augs
   - Train: HFlip, VFlip, Rotate(±10°), ShiftScaleRotate(shift≤0.1, scale 0.9–1.1), ColorJitter(0.1), Cutout(1×8).
   - Valid/Test: Resize+Normalize only; TTA: +hflip (±vflip).
5) Train
   - AdamW; head LR 1e-3, backbone 1e-4; OneCycle to max LR ~3e-3(head)/3e-4(backbone), 12 epochs, bs=256, early stopping.
6) Inference
   - For each test image: if exact-hash in train, use that label; else average fold logits (with TTA), sigmoid, average across folds.
7) Checks
   - Report per-fold/OOF AUC; verify no duplicate leakage across folds. Save OOF/test preds.

This path is fast, robust, and medal-proven. If OOF <0.9995 after duplicate grouping and the above config, add 2 extra seeds or a second lightweight backbone and average.


**Independent Kaggle Coach Feedback:** How to medal: Hit ≥0.9995 OOF AUC fast, then push to 1.000 with fold/seed ensembling, TTA, and (if needed) pseudo-labeling. Prioritize small/efficient models, conservative aug, airtight CV, and flawless submission.

What to execute now
- Data/validation
  - Verify files, IDs, image shapes; 32×32 RGB.
  - Stratified KFold with fixed seeds; start with 5 folds, move to 10 folds if OOF stalls <0.9995.
  - Compute dataset mean/std for normalization; don’t use ImageNet stats blindly.
  - Track OOF logits and per-fold AUC; seed everything for determinism.

- Models (train at least two in parallel)
  - ResNet18 pretrained, head → single logit (BCEWithLogitsLoss).
  - Simple custom CNN tailored for 32×32 (3–4 conv blocks + GAP + FC) to reduce overfit and add diversity.
  - Upscale input to 64–128; if using pretrained backbones, you can upscale to 224.
  - If OOF <0.9995, add a third lightweight backbone (EfficientNet-B0 or ConvNeXt-Tiny).

- Training setup
  - Augmentations: h/v flips, small rotations (±5–10°), slight shift/scale. Keep color jitter minimal. Optional light MixUp/CutMix.
  - Optimizer/schedule: AdamW (lr≈3e-4, wd≈1e-4), cosine or OneCycle; 10–20 epochs with early stopping on val AUC.
  - Regularization: label smoothing ~0.1; dropout 0.2–0.3 in head.
  - Batch size ≥32; stratified batches.

- Ensembling and boosting
  - Use KFold; average logits across folds before sigmoid.
  - Add 2 seeds per model; ensemble across seeds and backbones, weight by each model’s OOF AUC.
  - TTA at inference: original, hflip, vflip, and 90° rotations; average per image.
  - If still <1.000: pseudo-label high-confidence test images (e.g., p>0.99 or <0.01), retrain final ensemble using full train + pseudo-labels.

- Final training and submission
  - After CV, retrain top model(s) on 100% train for final blend.
  - Produce submission with probabilities (not hard labels), columns: id, has_cactus; order/ids match sample_submission.
  - Clip probs to [0.001, 0.999] to avoid extremes if needed.

Common pitfalls to avoid
- Data/ID mismatches or unsorted submission; wrong headers or using logits instead of probs.
- Non-stratified splits, data leakage (e.g., using test stats), or nondeterministic runs.
- Over-augmentation, too many epochs, or tiny batches causing noisy training.
- Ignoring class imbalance; consider BCE class weights or balanced sampler if needed.

Readiness checklist (stop only when all true)
- OOF AUC ≥0.9995 across folds; stable across seeds.
- Public LB ≈ 1.000 with fold/seed ensemble + TTA.
- Clean submission validation (shape, order, prob range).
- Logs saved: OOF predictions, per-fold metrics, and seeds for reproducibility.

=== TURN 0008 | 2025-09-12T16:42:30.133285 ===
LLM_OUTPUT:
**Planning hash computation**

I need to add a step to compute hashes for duplicate detection based on expert advice. This involves coding to compute SHA1 on raw bytes for all images in the training and testing datasets, and then saving the results in parquet or CSV for future use. I’m also considering computing perceptual hash (phash) with imagehash, even though it might be slower. Plus, I want to prioritize efficient reading. I'll structure these tasks accordingly, particularly for grouped cross-validation later.**Creating hash computations**

I’m planning how to handle the images for both training and testing. First, I’ll iterate over the images while logging progress. I’ll compute the SHA1 hex for the bytes and consider calculating the perceptual hash (phash) using imagehash if time allows. Next, I need to create DataFrames for `train_hashes` and `test_hashes` with relevant columns. I’ll also find exact hash matches to estimate potential leakage, saving the results as CSV or parquet files. Lastly, I’ll summarize exact matches for quick insight before moving on to model training.
{
  "tool": "edit_notebook",
  "filepath": "main.ipynb",
  "file_edit_payload": {
    "action": "insert_cell",
    "cell_index": 2,
    "cell_type": "code",
    "source": [
      "# Compute exact (SHA1) and perceptual (phash) hashes; detect duplicates/leakage",
      "import pandas as pd",
      "from pathlib import Path",
      "from PIL import Image",
      "import imagehash, hashlib, time, os, gc",
      "",
      "def sha1_file(path: Path) -> str:",
      "    with open(path, 'rb') as f:",
      "        return hashlib.sha1(f.read()).hexdigest()",
      "",
      "def phash_file(path: Path) -> str:",
      "    with Image.open(path) as im:",
      "        return str(imagehash.phash(im))  # 16-char hex",
      "",
      "def compute_hashes(img_dir: Path, ids: list[str], do_phash: bool = True, log_every: int = 1000):",
      "    rows = []",
      "    t0 = time.time()",
      "    for i, img_id in enumerate(ids):",
      "        p = img_dir / img_id",
      "        sha1 = sha1_file(p)",
      "        ph = phash_file(p) if do_phash else ''",
      "        rows.append((img_id, sha1, ph))",
      "        if (i+1) % log_every == 0:",
      "            print(f\"[hash] {i+1}/{len(ids)} processed; elapsed {time.time()-t0:.1f}s\", flush=True)",
      "    df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=['id','sha1','phash'])",
      "    return df",
      "",
      "# Load IDs",
      "train_df = pd.read_csv('train.csv')",
      "test_df = pd.read_csv('sample_submission.csv')",
      "train_ids = train_df['id'].tolist()",
      "test_ids = test_df['id'].tolist()",
      "",
      "t0 = time.time()",
      "train_hash_df = compute_hashes(Path('train'), train_ids, do_phash=True, log_every=2000)",
      "train_hash_df = train_hash_df.merge(train_df, on='id', how='left')",
      "print(f\"[hash] train hashing done in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s; rows={len(train_hash_df)}\")",
      "",
      "t1 = time.time()",
      "test_hash_df = compute_hashes(Path('test'), test_ids, do_phash=True, log_every=1000)",
      "print(f\"[hash] test hashing done in {time.time()-t1:.1f}s; rows={len(test_hash_df)}\")",
      "",
      "# Save for reuse",
      "train_hash_df.to_csv('train_hashes.csv', index=False)",
      "test_hash_df.to_csv('test_hashes.csv', index=False)",
      "print('[hash] saved train_hashes.csv and test_hashes.csv')",
      "",
      "# Duplicate analysis in train",
      "dup_counts = train_hash_df.groupby('sha1').size().reset_index(name='n')",
      "train_dups = dup_counts[dup_counts['n'] > 1]['sha1']",
      "print(f\"[dups] exact-duplicate groups in train: {len(train_dups)} (total dup images: {int(dup_counts['n'].sum() - (dup_counts['n']>0).sum())})\")",
      "",
      "# Check for label conflicts within duplicate groups",
      "conflicts = (train_hash_df.groupby('sha1')['has_cactus']",
      "             .nunique().reset_index(name='n_labels'))",
      "conflicts = conflicts[conflicts['n_labels'] > 1]",
      "print(f\"[dups] label conflicts across identical images: {len(conflicts)}\")",
      "if len(conflicts):",
      "    print(conflicts.head())",
      "",
      "# Test-train leakage via exact hash",
      "test_leak = test_hash_df.merge(train_hash_df[['sha1','has_cactus']].drop_duplicates('sha1'), on='sha1', how='left')",
      "n_match = test_leak['has_cactus'].notna().sum()",
      "print(f\"[leak] test images with exact-hash match to train: {n_match} / {len(test_hash_df)} ({n_match/len(test_hash_df)*100:.1f}%)\")",
      "",
      "# Build lookup map (sha1 -> label) for inference; prefer majority label if duplicates exist",
      "label_by_sha1 = (train_hash_df.groupby('sha1')['has_cactus']",
      "                 .mean().round().astype(int).to_dict())",
      "import json",
      "with open('train_sha1_label_map.json', 'w') as f:",
      "    json.dump(label_by_sha1, f)",
      "print('[leak] saved train_sha1_label_map.json (sha1 -> majority label)')",
      "",
      "# Prepare groups for CV: use exact sha1 as group",
      "train_hash_df[['id','sha1','has_cactus']].to_csv('cv_groups.csv', index=False)",
      "print('[cv] saved cv_groups.csv (id, sha1, has_cactus) for StratifiedGroupKFold')",
      "",
      "gc.collect();"
    ]

      "    def __init__(self, img_dir, df, train=True, tta_flip=None):",

      "        self.df = df.reset_index(drop=True)",
      "        self.train = train",
      "        self.tta_flip = tta_flip  # None or 'hflip'",
      "        self.tfms = get_transforms(train=train)",
      "    def __len__(self):",

      "    def __getitem__(self, idx):",
      "        row = self.df.iloc[idx]",

      "        img_t = torch.from_numpy(aug['image'].transpose(2,0,1)).float()",

      "            y = torch.tensor(row['has_cactus'], dtype=torch.float32)",

      "        if val_auc > best_auc:",
      "            best_auc = val_auc; best_state = {k: v.cpu() for k,v in model.state_dict().items()}; no_improve = 0",
      "        else:",
      "            no_improve += 1",
      "        if no_improve >= 3:",
      "            print(f\"[fold {fold}] Early stopping at epoch {epoch}\")",
      "            break",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] best val AUC: {best_auc:.6f} | fold time {time.time()-t_fold0:.1f}s\")",
      "    all_fold_aucs.append(best_auc)",
      "    # Load best and infer OOF",
      "    model.load_state_dict({k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in best_state.items()}, strict=True)",
      "    # OOF preds with best",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    _, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "    oof[va_idx] = val_preds",

      "    p1, ids = predict_loader(model, test_loader)",
      "    test_ds_h = CactusDataset('test', test_ids, train=False, tta_flip='hflip')",
      "    test_loader_h = DataLoader(test_ds_h, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    p2, _ = predict_loader(model, test_loader_h)",
      "    p = (p1 + p2) / 2.0",
      "    test_pred_accum += p.astype(np.float32)",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] test inference done.\")",
      "    del model, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler, tr_loader, va_loader, test_loader, test_loader_h",
      "    gc.collect(); torch.cuda.empty_cache()",
      "",
      "print(f\"OOF AUC: {roc_auc_score(train_df['has_cactus'].values, oof):.6f}\")",
      "print(\"Fold AUCs:\", [f\"{a:.6f}\" for a in all_fold_aucs])",
      "",
      "# Save OOF",
      "np.save('oof_preds.npy', oof)",
      "pd.DataFrame({'id': train_df['id'], 'oof_pred': oof, 'has_cactus': train_df['has_cactus']}).to_csv('oof_preds.csv', index=False)",

      "test_pred = test_pred_accum / N_FOLDS",

      "sub = sub.merge(lookup[['id','label_from_train']], on='id', how='left')",
      "sub['pred'] = test_pred",
      "sub['has_cactus'] = np.where(sub['label_from_train'].notna(), sub['label_from_train'], sub['pred'])",
      "submission = sub[['id','has_cactus']].copy()",
      "submission.to_csv('submission.csv', index=False)",
      "print('Saved submission.csv with shape', submission.shape)"
    ]

      "# ResNet18@96 with StratifiedGroupKFold, OOF AUC, TTA, and submission",

      "for env_key in ['HF_HOME', 'HUGGINGFACE_HUB_CACHE', 'TORCH_HOME', 'TIMM_CACHE_DIR']:",
      "    os.environ[env_key] = str(CACHE_DIR)",

      "IMG_SIZE = 96",
      "N_FOLDS = 5",
      "EPOCHS = 12",
      "BATCH_SIZE = 256",
      "WORKERS = 2",
      "LR_HEAD = 1e-3",
      "LR_BACKBONE = 1e-4",
      "WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4",
      "DEVICE = 'cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'",
      "",
      "def set_seed(seed=SEED):",
      "    random.seed(seed); np.random.seed(seed); torch.manual_seed(seed); torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)",
      "    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True",
      "    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False",
      "",
      "set_seed()",
      "",
      "IMAGENET_MEAN = (0.485, 0.456, 0.406)",
      "IMAGENET_STD = (0.229, 0.224, 0.225)",
      "",
      "def get_transforms(train=True):",
      "    if train:",
      "        return A.Compose([",
      "            A.Resize(IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE, interpolation=3),",
      "            A.HorizontalFlip(p=0.5),",
      "            A.VerticalFlip(p=0.5),",
      "            A.ShiftScaleRotate(shift_limit=0.1, scale_limit=0.1, rotate_limit=10, border_mode=0, p=0.6),",
      "            A.ColorJitter(brightness=0.1, contrast=0.1, saturation=0.0, hue=0.0, p=0.2),",
      "            # Dropout/Cutout removed to avoid version arg mismatch; add later if needed",
      "            A.Normalize(mean=IMAGENET_MEAN, std=IMAGENET_STD),",
      "        ])",
      "    else:",
      "        return A.Compose([",
      "            A.Resize(IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE, interpolation=3),",
      "            A.Normalize(mean=IMAGENET_MEAN, std=IMAGENET_STD),",
      "        ])",
      "",
      "class CactusDataset(Dataset):",
      "    def __init__(self, img_dir, df, train=True, tta_flip=None):",
      "        self.img_dir = Path(img_dir)",
      "        self.df = df.reset_index(drop=True)",
      "        self.train = train",
      "        self.tta_flip = tta_flip  # None or 'hflip'",
      "        self.tfms = get_transforms(train=train)",
      "    def __len__(self):",
      "        return len(self.df)",
      "    def __getitem__(self, idx):",
      "        row = self.df.iloc[idx]",
      "        img_path = self.img_dir / row['id']",
      "        with Image.open(img_path) as im:",
      "            im = im.convert('RGB')",
      "            img = np.array(im)",
      "        if self.tta_flip == 'hflip':",
      "            img = np.ascontiguousarray(img[:, ::-1, :])",
      "        aug = self.tfms(image=img)",
      "        img_t = torch.from_numpy(aug['image'].transpose(2,0,1)).float()",
      "        if self.train and 'has_cactus' in row:",
      "            y = torch.tensor(row['has_cactus'], dtype=torch.float32)",
      "            return img_t, y",
      "        return img_t, row['id']",
      "",
      "def build_model():",
      "    model = timm.create_model('resnet18', pretrained=True, num_classes=1)",
      "    return model",
      "",
      "def make_optimizer(model):",
      "    head_params, backbone_params = [], []",
      "    head_names = ['fc']",
      "    for n, p in model.named_parameters():",
      "        if any([n.startswith(hn) or f'.{hn}.' in n for hn in head_names]):",
      "            head_params.append(p)",
      "        else:",
      "            backbone_params.append(p)",
      "    opt = torch.optim.AdamW([",
      "        {'params': backbone_params, 'lr': LR_BACKBONE},",
      "        {'params': head_params, 'lr': LR_HEAD},",
      "    ], weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY)",
      "    return opt",
      "",
      "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler):",
      "    model.train()",
      "    running = 0.0",
      "    for i, (x, y) in enumerate(loader):",
      "        x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True); y = y.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True).view(-1,1)",
      "        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)",
      "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=(DEVICE=='cuda')):",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            loss = loss_fn(logits, y)",
      "        scaler.scale(loss).backward()",
      "        scaler.step(optimizer); scaler.update()",
      "        running += loss.item()*x.size(0)",
      "        if (i+1) % 20 == 0:",
      "            print(f\"  [train] step {i+1}/{len(loader)} loss={running/((i+1)*loader.batch_size):.4f}\", flush=True)",
      "    return running/len(loader.dataset)",
      "",
      "def valid_one_epoch(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    preds, targs = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, y in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            targs.append(y.numpy())",
      "    preds = np.concatenate(preds); targs = np.concatenate(targs)",
      "    auc = roc_auc_score(targs, preds)",
      "    return auc, preds",
      "",
      "def predict_loader(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    out_ids, out_preds = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, ids in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            out_preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            out_ids += list(ids)",
      "    return np.concatenate(out_preds), out_ids",
      "",
      "# Load data and groups",
      "train_df = pd.read_csv('train.csv')",
      "groups_df = pd.read_csv('cv_groups.csv')",
      "train_df = train_df.merge(groups_df[['id','sha1']], on='id', how='left')",
      "test_ids = pd.read_csv('sample_submission.csv')",
      "",
      "skf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)",
      "oof = np.zeros(len(train_df), dtype=np.float32)",
      "test_pred_accum = np.zeros(len(test_ids), dtype=np.float32)",
      "",
      "all_fold_aucs = []",
      "fold_start = time.time()",
      "for fold, (tr_idx, va_idx) in enumerate(skf.split(train_df['id'], train_df['has_cactus'], groups=train_df['sha1'])):",
      "    print(f\"===== Fold {fold+1}/{N_FOLDS} =====\")",
      "    tr_df = train_df.iloc[tr_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    va_df = train_df.iloc[va_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    print(f\"train size: {len(tr_df)} | valid size: {len(va_df)}\")",
      "",
      "    tr_ds = CactusDataset('train', tr_df, train=True)",
      "    va_ds = CactusDataset('train', va_df, train=False)",
      "    tr_loader = DataLoader(tr_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True, drop_last=True)",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "",
      "    model = build_model().to(DEVICE)",
      "    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)",
      "    loss_fn = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss()",
      "    scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=(DEVICE=='cuda'))",
      "",
      "    best_auc = -1.0; best_state = None; no_improve = 0",
      "    t_fold0 = time.time()",
      "    for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS+1):",
      "        t0 = time.time()",
      "        tr_loss = train_one_epoch(model, tr_loader, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler)",
      "        val_auc, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "        elapsed = time.time()-t0",
      "        print(f\"[fold {fold}] epoch {epoch:02d} | tr_loss {tr_loss:.4f} | val_auc {val_auc:.6f} | {elapsed:.1f}s\", flush=True)",
      "        if val_auc > best_auc:",
      "            best_auc = val_auc; best_state = {k: v.cpu() for k,v in model.state_dict().items()}; no_improve = 0",
      "        else:",
      "            no_improve += 1",
      "        if no_improve >= 3:",
      "            print(f\"[fold {fold}] Early stopping at epoch {epoch}\")",
      "            break",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] best val AUC: {best_auc:.6f} | fold time {time.time()-t_fold0:.1f}s\")",
      "    all_fold_aucs.append(best_auc)",
      "    # Load best and infer OOF",
      "    model.load_state_dict({k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in best_state.items()}, strict=True)",
      "    # OOF preds with best",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    _, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "    oof[va_idx] = val_preds",
      "",
      "    # Test inference with simple TTA (orig + hflip)",
      "    test_ds = CactusDataset('test', test_ids, train=False, tta_flip=None)",
      "    test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    p1, ids = predict_loader(model, test_loader)",
      "    test_ds_h = CactusDataset('test', test_ids, train=False, tta_flip='hflip')",
      "    test_loader_h = DataLoader(test_ds_h, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    p2, _ = predict_loader(model, test_loader_h)",
      "    p = (p1 + p2) / 2.0",
      "    test_pred_accum += p.astype(np.float32)",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] test inference done.\")",
      "    del model, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler, tr_loader, va_loader, test_loader, test_loader_h",
      "    gc.collect(); torch.cuda.empty_cache()",
      "",
      "print(f\"OOF AUC: {roc_auc_score(train_df['has_cactus'].values, oof):.6f}\")",
      "print(\"Fold AUCs:\", [f\"{a:.6f}\" for a in all_fold_aucs])",
      "",
      "# Save OOF",
      "np.save('oof_preds.npy', oof)",
      "pd.DataFrame({'id': train_df['id'], 'oof_pred': oof, 'has_cactus': train_df['has_cactus']}).to_csv('oof_preds.csv', index=False)",
      "",
      "# Average across folds",
      "test_pred = test_pred_accum / N_FOLDS",
      "",
      "# Optional: exact-hash lookup (none expected per analysis, but keep logic) -> override predictions if match found",
      "with open('train_sha1_label_map.json', 'r') as f:",
      "    sha1_to_label = json.load(f)",
      "test_hash_df = pd.read_csv('test_hashes.csv')",
      "lookup = test_hash_df[['id','sha1']].copy()",
      "lookup['label_from_train'] = lookup['sha1'].map(sha1_to_label).astype('float32')",
      "overrides = lookup['label_from_train'].notna().sum()",
      "print(f\"[inference] exact-hash overrides in test: {overrides}\")",
      "",
      "sub = test_ids.copy()",
      "sub = sub.merge(lookup[['id','label_from_train']], on='id', how='left')",
      "sub['pred'] = test_pred",
      "sub['has_cactus'] = np.where(sub['label_from_train'].notna(), sub['label_from_train'], sub['pred'])",
      "submission = sub[['id','has_cactus']].copy()",
      "submission.to_csv('submission.csv', index=False)",
      "print('Saved submission.csv with shape', submission.shape)"
    ]
  },

      "# ResNet18@96 with StratifiedGroupKFold, OOF AUC, TTA, and submission",

      "for env_key in ['HF_HOME', 'HUGGINGFACE_HUB_CACHE', 'TORCH_HOME', 'TIMM_CACHE_DIR', 'XDG_CACHE_HOME', 'TRANSFORMERS_CACHE']:",
      "    os.environ[env_key] = str(CACHE_DIR)",

      "IMG_SIZE = 96",
      "N_FOLDS = 5",
      "EPOCHS = 12",
      "BATCH_SIZE = 256",
      "WORKERS = 2",
      "LR = 3e-3",
      "WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4",
      "DEVICE = 'cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'",
      "",
      "def set_seed(seed=SEED):",
      "    random.seed(seed); np.random.seed(seed); torch.manual_seed(seed); torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)",
      "    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True",
      "    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False",
      "",
      "set_seed()",
      "",
      "IMAGENET_MEAN = (0.485, 0.456, 0.406)",
      "IMAGENET_STD = (0.229, 0.224, 0.225)",
      "",
      "def get_transforms(train=True):",
      "    if train:",
      "        return A.Compose([",
      "            A.Resize(IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE, interpolation=3),",
      "            A.HorizontalFlip(p=0.5),",
      "            A.VerticalFlip(p=0.5),",
      "            A.ShiftScaleRotate(shift_limit=0.1, scale_limit=0.1, rotate_limit=10, border_mode=0, p=0.6),",
      "            A.ColorJitter(brightness=0.1, contrast=0.1, saturation=0.0, hue=0.0, p=0.2),",
      "            A.Normalize(mean=IMAGENET_MEAN, std=IMAGENET_STD),",
      "        ])",
      "    else:",
      "        return A.Compose([",
      "            A.Resize(IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE, interpolation=3),",
      "            A.Normalize(mean=IMAGENET_MEAN, std=IMAGENET_STD),",
      "        ])",
      "",
      "class CactusDataset(Dataset):",
      "    def __init__(self, img_dir, df, train=True, tta_flip=None):",
      "        self.img_dir = Path(img_dir)",
      "        self.df = df.reset_index(drop=True)",
      "        self.train = train",
      "        self.tta_flip = tta_flip  # None or 'hflip'",
      "        self.tfms = get_transforms(train=train)",
      "    def __len__(self):",
      "        return len(self.df)",
      "    def __getitem__(self, idx):",
      "        row = self.df.iloc[idx]",
      "        img_path = self.img_dir / row['id']",
      "        with Image.open(img_path) as im:",
      "            im = im.convert('RGB')",
      "            img = np.array(im)",
      "        if self.tta_flip == 'hflip':",
      "            img = np.ascontiguousarray(img[:, ::-1, :])",
      "        aug = self.tfms(image=img)",
      "        img_t = torch.from_numpy(aug['image'].transpose(2,0,1)).float()",
      "        if self.train and 'has_cactus' in row:",
      "            y = torch.tensor(row['has_cactus'], dtype=torch.float32)",
      "            return img_t, y",
      "        return img_t, row['id']",
      "",
      "class SmallCNN(nn.Module):",
      "    def __init__(self):",
      "        super().__init__()",
      "        def block(cin, cout):",
      "            return nn.Sequential(",
      "                nn.Conv2d(cin, cout, 3, padding=1),",
      "                nn.BatchNorm2d(cout),",
      "                nn.ReLU(inplace=True),",
      "                nn.Conv2d(cout, cout, 3, padding=1),",
      "                nn.BatchNorm2d(cout),",
      "                nn.ReLU(inplace=True),",
      "            )",
      "        self.stem = nn.Sequential(",
      "            nn.Conv2d(3, 32, 3, padding=1),",
      "            nn.BatchNorm2d(32),",
      "            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),",
      "        )",
      "        self.block1 = block(32, 64)",
      "        self.pool1 = nn.MaxPool2d(2)  # 96->48",
      "        self.block2 = block(64, 128)",
      "        self.pool2 = nn.MaxPool2d(2)  # 48->24",
      "        self.block3 = block(128, 256)",
      "        self.pool3 = nn.MaxPool2d(2)  # 24->12",
      "        self.block4 = block(256, 256)",
      "        self.pool4 = nn.AdaptiveAvgPool2d(1)",
      "        self.head = nn.Sequential(",
      "            nn.Dropout(0.3),",
      "            nn.Linear(256, 1),",
      "        )",
      "    def forward(self, x):",
      "        x = self.stem(x)",
      "        x = self.pool1(self.block1(x))",
      "        x = self.pool2(self.block2(x))",
      "        x = self.pool3(self.block3(x))",
      "        x = self.pool4(self.block4(x)).flatten(1)",
      "        x = self.head(x)",
      "        return x",
      "",
      "def build_model():",
      "    return SmallCNN()",
      "",
      "def make_optimizer(model):",
      "    return torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY)",
      "",
      "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler):",
      "    model.train()",
      "    running = 0.0",
      "    for i, (x, y) in enumerate(loader):",
      "        x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True); y = y.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True).view(-1,1)",
      "        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)",
      "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=(DEVICE=='cuda')):",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            loss = loss_fn(logits, y)",
      "        scaler.scale(loss).backward()",
      "        scaler.step(optimizer); scaler.update()",
      "        running += loss.item()*x.size(0)",
      "        if (i+1) % 20 == 0:",
      "            print(f\"  [train] step {i+1}/{len(loader)} loss={running/((i+1)*loader.batch_size):.4f}\", flush=True)",
      "    return running/len(loader.dataset)",
      "",
      "def valid_one_epoch(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    preds, targs = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, y in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            targs.append(y.numpy())",
      "    preds = np.concatenate(preds); targs = np.concatenate(targs)",
      "    auc = roc_auc_score(targs, preds)",
      "    return auc, preds",
      "",
      "def predict_loader(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    out_ids, out_preds = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, ids in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            out_preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            out_ids += list(ids)",
      "    return np.concatenate(out_preds), out_ids",
      "",
      "# Load data and groups",
      "train_df = pd.read_csv('train.csv')",
      "groups_df = pd.read_csv('cv_groups.csv')",
      "train_df = train_df.merge(groups_df[['id','sha1']], on='id', how='left')",
      "test_ids = pd.read_csv('sample_submission.csv')",
      "",
      "skf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)",
      "oof = np.zeros(len(train_df), dtype=np.float32)",
      "test_pred_accum = np.zeros(len(test_ids), dtype=np.float32)",
      "",
      "all_fold_aucs = []",
      "fold_start = time.time()",
      "for fold, (tr_idx, va_idx) in enumerate(skf.split(train_df['id'], train_df['has_cactus'], groups=train_df['sha1'])):",
      "    print(f\"===== Fold {fold+1}/{N_FOLDS} =====\")",
      "    tr_df = train_df.iloc[tr_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    va_df = train_df.iloc[va_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    print(f\"train size: {len(tr_df)} | valid size: {len(va_df)}\")",
      "",
      "    tr_ds = CactusDataset('train', tr_df, train=True)",
      "    va_ds = CactusDataset('train', va_df, train=False)",
      "    tr_loader = DataLoader(tr_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True, drop_last=True)",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "",
      "    model = build_model().to(DEVICE)",
      "    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)",
      "    loss_fn = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss()",
      "    scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=(DEVICE=='cuda'))",
      "",
      "    best_auc = -1.0; best_state = None; no_improve = 0",
      "    t_fold0 = time.time()",
      "    for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS+1):",
      "        t0 = time.time()",
      "        tr_loss = train_one_epoch(model, tr_loader, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler)",
      "        val_auc, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "        elapsed = time.time()-t0",
      "        print(f\"[fold {fold}] epoch {epoch:02d} | tr_loss {tr_loss:.4f} | val_auc {val_auc:.6f} | {elapsed:.1f}s\", flush=True)",
      "        if val_auc > best_auc:",
      "            best_auc = val_auc; best_state = {k: v.cpu() for k,v in model.state_dict().items()}; no_improve = 0",
      "        else:",
      "            no_improve += 1",
      "        if no_improve >= 3:",
      "            print(f\"[fold {fold}] Early stopping at epoch {epoch}\")",
      "            break",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] best val AUC: {best_auc:.6f} | fold time {time.time()-t_fold0:.1f}s\")",
      "    all_fold_aucs.append(best_auc)",
      "    # Load best and infer OOF",
      "    model.load_state_dict({k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in best_state.items()}, strict=True)",
      "    # OOF preds with best",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    _, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "    oof[va_idx] = val_preds",
      "",
      "    # Test inference with simple TTA (orig + hflip)",
      "    test_ds = CactusDataset('test', test_ids, train=False, tta_flip=None)",
      "    test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    p1, ids = predict_loader(model, test_loader)",
      "    test_ds_h = CactusDataset('test', test_ids, train=False, tta_flip='hflip')",
      "    test_loader_h = DataLoader(test_ds_h, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    p2, _ = predict_loader(model, test_loader_h)",
      "    p = (p1 + p2) / 2.0",
      "    test_pred_accum += p.astype(np.float32)",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] test inference done.\")",
      "    del model, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler, tr_loader, va_loader, test_loader, test_loader_h",
      "    gc.collect(); torch.cuda.empty_cache()",
      "",
      "print(f\"OOF AUC: {roc_auc_score(train_df['has_cactus'].values, oof):.6f}\")",
      "print(\"Fold AUCs:\", [f\"{a:.6f}\" for a in all_fold_aucs])",
      "",
      "# Save OOF",
      "np.save('oof_preds.npy', oof)",
      "pd.DataFrame({'id': train_df['id'], 'oof_pred': oof, 'has_cactus': train_df['has_cactus']}).to_csv('oof_preds.csv', index=False)",
      "",
      "# Average across folds",
      "test_pred = test_pred_accum / N_FOLDS",
      "",
      "# Exact-hash lookup (expected 0 overrides here)",
      "with open('train_sha1_label_map.json', 'r') as f:",
      "    sha1_to_label = json.load(f)",
      "test_hash_df = pd.read_csv('test_hashes.csv')",
      "lookup = test_hash_df[['id','sha1']].copy()",
      "lookup['label_from_train'] = lookup['sha1'].map(sha1_to_label).astype('float32')",
      "overrides = lookup['label_from_train'].notna().sum()",
      "print(f\"[inference] exact-hash overrides in test: {overrides}\")",
      "",
      "sub = test_ids.copy()",
      "sub = sub.merge(lookup[['id','label_from_train']], on='id', how='left')",
      "sub['pred'] = test_pred",
      "sub['has_cactus'] = np.where(sub['label_from_train'].notna(), sub['label_from_train'], sub['pred'])",
      "submission = sub[['id','has_cactus']].copy()",
      "submission.to_csv('submission.csv', index=False)",
      "print('Saved submission.csv with shape', submission.shape)"

      "import torch",

      "from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader",
      "import albumentations as A",
      "",
      "# Ensure writable caches for any libs that may try to use ~/.cache",
      "CACHE_DIR = Path('./.model_cache')",
      "for env_key in ['HF_HOME', 'HUGGINGFACE_HUB_CACHE', 'TORCH_HOME', 'TIMM_CACHE_DIR', 'XDG_CACHE_HOME', 'TRANSFORMERS_CACHE']:",
      "    os.environ[env_key] = str(CACHE_DIR)",
      "CACHE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)",
      "",
      "SEED = 42",
      "IMG_SIZE = 96",
      "N_FOLDS = 5",
      "EPOCHS = 12",
      "BATCH_SIZE = 256",
      "WORKERS = 2",
      "LR = 3e-3",
      "WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4",
      "DEVICE = 'cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'",
      "",
      "def set_seed(seed=SEED):",
      "    random.seed(seed); np.random.seed(seed); torch.manual_seed(seed); torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)",
      "    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True",
      "    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False",
      "",
      "set_seed()",
      "",
      "IMAGENET_MEAN = (0.485, 0.456, 0.406)",
      "IMAGENET_STD = (0.229, 0.224, 0.225)",
      "",
      "def get_transforms(train=True):",
      "    if train:",
      "        return A.Compose([",
      "            A.Resize(IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE, interpolation=3),",
      "            A.HorizontalFlip(p=0.5),",
      "            A.VerticalFlip(p=0.5),",
      "            A.ShiftScaleRotate(shift_limit=0.1, scale_limit=0.1, rotate_limit=10, border_mode=0, p=0.6),",
      "            A.ColorJitter(brightness=0.1, contrast=0.1, saturation=0.0, hue=0.0, p=0.2),",
      "            A.Normalize(mean=IMAGENET_MEAN, std=IMAGENET_STD),",
      "        ])",
      "    else:",
      "        return A.Compose([",
      "            A.Resize(IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE, interpolation=3),",
      "            A.Normalize(mean=IMAGENET_MEAN, std=IMAGENET_STD),",
      "        ])",
      "",
      "class CactusDataset(Dataset):",
      "    def __init__(self, img_dir, df, train=True, tta_flip=None):",
      "        self.img_dir = Path(img_dir)",
      "        self.df = df.reset_index(drop=True)",
      "        self.train = train",
      "        self.tta_flip = tta_flip  # None or 'hflip'",
      "        self.tfms = get_transforms(train=train)",
      "    def __len__(self):",
      "        return len(self.df)",
      "    def __getitem__(self, idx):",
      "        row = self.df.iloc[idx]",
      "        img_path = self.img_dir / row['id']",
      "        with Image.open(img_path) as im:",
      "            im = im.convert('RGB')",
      "            img = np.array(im)",
      "        if self.tta_flip == 'hflip':",
      "            img = np.ascontiguousarray(img[:, ::-1, :])",
      "        aug = self.tfms(image=img)",
      "        img_t = torch.from_numpy(aug['image'].transpose(2,0,1)).float()",
      "        if self.train:",
      "            y = torch.tensor(row['has_cactus'], dtype=torch.float32)",
      "            return img_t, y",
      "        return img_t, row['id']",
      "",
      "class SmallCNN(nn.Module):",
      "    def __init__(self):",
      "        super().__init__()",
      "        def block(cin, cout):",
      "            return nn.Sequential(",
      "                nn.Conv2d(cin, cout, 3, padding=1),",
      "                nn.BatchNorm2d(cout),",
      "                nn.ReLU(inplace=True),",
      "                nn.Conv2d(cout, cout, 3, padding=1),",
      "                nn.BatchNorm2d(cout),",
      "                nn.ReLU(inplace=True),",
      "            )",
      "        self.stem = nn.Sequential(",
      "            nn.Conv2d(3, 32, 3, padding=1),",
      "            nn.BatchNorm2d(32),",
      "            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),",
      "        )",
      "        self.block1 = block(32, 64)",
      "        self.pool1 = nn.MaxPool2d(2)  # 96->48",
      "        self.block2 = block(64, 128)",
      "        self.pool2 = nn.MaxPool2d(2)  # 48->24",
      "        self.block3 = block(128, 256)",
      "        self.pool3 = nn.MaxPool2d(2)  # 24->12",
      "        self.block4 = block(256, 256)",
      "        self.pool4 = nn.AdaptiveAvgPool2d(1)",
      "        self.head = nn.Sequential(",
      "            nn.Dropout(0.3),",
      "            nn.Linear(256, 1),",
      "        )",
      "    def forward(self, x):",
      "        x = self.stem(x)",
      "        x = self.pool1(self.block1(x))",
      "        x = self.pool2(self.block2(x))",
      "        x = self.pool3(self.block3(x))",
      "        x = self.pool4(self.block4(x)).flatten(1)",
      "        x = self.head(x)",
      "        return x",
      "",
      "def build_model():",
      "    return SmallCNN()",
      "",
      "def make_optimizer(model):",
      "    return torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY)",
      "",
      "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler):",
      "    model.train()",
      "    running = 0.0",
      "    for i, (x, y) in enumerate(loader):",
      "        x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True); y = y.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True).view(-1,1)",
      "        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)",
      "        with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=(DEVICE=='cuda')):",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            loss = loss_fn(logits, y)",
      "        scaler.scale(loss).backward()",
      "        scaler.step(optimizer); scaler.update()",
      "        running += loss.item()*x.size(0)",
      "        if (i+1) % 20 == 0:",
      "            print(f\"  [train] step {i+1}/{len(loader)} loss={running/((i+1)*loader.batch_size):.4f}\", flush=True)",
      "    return running/len(loader.dataset)",
      "",
      "def valid_one_epoch(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    preds, targs = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, y in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            targs.append(y.numpy())",
      "    preds = np.concatenate(preds); targs = np.concatenate(targs)",
      "    auc = roc_auc_score(targs, preds)",
      "    return auc, preds",
      "",
      "def predict_loader(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    out_ids, out_preds = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, ids in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            out_preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            out_ids += list(ids)",
      "    return np.concatenate(out_preds), out_ids",
      "",
      "# Load data and groups",
      "train_df = pd.read_csv('train.csv')",
      "groups_df = pd.read_csv('cv_groups.csv')",
      "train_df = train_df.merge(groups_df[['id','sha1']], on='id', how='left')",
      "test_ids = pd.read_csv('sample_submission.csv')",
      "",
      "skf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)",
      "oof = np.zeros(len(train_df), dtype=np.float32)",
      "test_pred_accum = np.zeros(len(test_ids), dtype=np.float32)",
      "",
      "all_fold_aucs = []",
      "fold_start = time.time()",
      "for fold, (tr_idx, va_idx) in enumerate(skf.split(train_df['id'], train_df['has_cactus'], groups=train_df['sha1'])):",
      "    print(f\"===== Fold {fold+1}/{N_FOLDS} =====\")",
      "    tr_df = train_df.iloc[tr_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    va_df = train_df.iloc[va_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    print(f\"train size: {len(tr_df)} | valid size: {len(va_df)}\")",
      "",
      "    tr_ds = CactusDataset('train', tr_df, train=True)",
      "    va_ds = CactusDataset('train', va_df, train=False)",
      "    tr_loader = DataLoader(tr_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True, drop_last=True)",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "",
      "    model = build_model().to(DEVICE)",
      "    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)",
      "    loss_fn = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss()",
      "    scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=(DEVICE=='cuda'))",
      "",
      "    best_auc = -1.0; best_state = None; no_improve = 0",
      "    t_fold0 = time.time()",
      "    for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS+1):",
      "        t0 = time.time()",
      "        tr_loss = train_one_epoch(model, tr_loader, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler)",
      "        val_auc, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "        elapsed = time.time()-t0",
      "        print(f\"[fold {fold}] epoch {epoch:02d} | tr_loss {tr_loss:.4f} | val_auc {val_auc:.6f} | {elapsed:.1f}s\", flush=True)",
      "        if val_auc > best_auc:",
      "            best_auc = val_auc; best_state = {k: v.cpu() for k,v in model.state_dict().items()}; no_improve = 0",
      "        else:",
      "            no_improve += 1",
      "        if no_improve >= 3:",
      "            print(f\"[fold {fold}] Early stopping at epoch {epoch}\")",
      "            break",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] best val AUC: {best_auc:.6f} | fold time {time.time()-t_fold0:.1f}s\")",
      "    all_fold_aucs.append(best_auc)",
      "    # Load best and infer OOF",
      "    model.load_state_dict({k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in best_state.items()}, strict=True)",
      "    # OOF preds with best",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    _, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "    oof[va_idx] = val_preds",
      "",
      "    # Test inference with simple TTA (orig + hflip)",
      "    test_ds = CactusDataset('test', test_ids, train=False, tta_flip=None)",
      "    test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    p1, ids = predict_loader(model, test_loader)",
      "    test_ds_h = CactusDataset('test', test_ids, train=False, tta_flip='hflip')",
      "    test_loader_h = DataLoader(test_ds_h, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    p2, _ = predict_loader(model, test_loader_h)",
      "    p = (p1 + p2) / 2.0",
      "    test_pred_accum += p.astype(np.float32)",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] test inference done.\")",
      "    del model, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler, tr_loader, va_loader, test_loader, test_loader_h",
      "    gc.collect(); torch.cuda.empty_cache()",
      "",
      "print(f\"OOF AUC: {roc_auc_score(train_df['has_cactus'].values, oof):.6f}\")",
      "print(\"Fold AUCs:\", [f\"{a:.6f}\" for a in all_fold_aucs])",
      "",
      "# Save OOF",
      "np.save('oof_preds.npy', oof)",
      "pd.DataFrame({'id': train_df['id'], 'oof_pred': oof, 'has_cactus': train_df['has_cactus']}).to_csv('oof_preds.csv', index=False)",
      "",
      "# Average across folds",
      "test_pred = test_pred_accum / N_FOLDS",
      "",
      "# Exact-hash lookup (expected 0 overrides here)",
      "with open('train_sha1_label_map.json', 'r') as f:",
      "    sha1_to_label = json.load(f)",
      "test_hash_df = pd.read_csv('test_hashes.csv')",
      "lookup = test_hash_df[['id','sha1']].copy()",
      "lookup['label_from_train'] = lookup['sha1'].map(sha1_to_label).astype('float32')",
      "overrides = lookup['label_from_train'].notna().sum()",
      "print(f\"[inference] exact-hash overrides in test: {overrides}\")",
      "",
      "sub = test_ids.copy()",
      "sub = sub.merge(lookup[['id','label_from_train']], on='id', how='left')",
      "sub['pred'] = test_pred",
      "sub['has_cactus'] = np.where(sub['label_from_train'].notna(), sub['label_from_train'], sub['pred'])",
      "submission = sub[['id','has_cactus']].copy()",
      "submission.to_csv('submission.csv', index=False)",
      "print('Saved submission.csv with shape', submission.shape)"
    ],
    "cell_type": "code"
  },
  "explanation": "Replaced timm ResNet18 (which failed due to read-only cache during pretrained download) with a custom SmallCNN trained from scratch at 96px.       "    return running/len(loader.dataset)",
      "",
      "def valid_one_epoch(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    preds, targs = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, y in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True); y = y.view(-1,1)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            targs.append(y.squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "    preds = np.concatenate(preds); targs = np.concatenate(targs)",
      "    auc = roc_auc_score(targs, preds)",
      "    return auc, preds",
      "",
      "def predict_loader(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    out_ids, out_preds = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, ids in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            out_preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            out_ids += list(ids)",
      "    return np.concatenate(out_preds), out_ids",
      "",
      "# Load data and groups",
      "train_df = pd.read_csv('train.csv')",
      "groups_df = pd.read_csv('cv_groups.csv')",
      "train_df = train_df.merge(groups_df[['id','sha1']], on='id', how='left')",
      "test_ids = pd.read_csv('sample_submission.csv')",
      "",
      "skf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)",
      "oof = np.zeros(len(train_df), dtype=np.float32)",
      "test_pred_accum = np.zeros(len(test_ids), dtype=np.float32)",
      "",
      "all_fold_aucs = []",
      "fold_start = time.time()",
      "for fold, (tr_idx, va_idx) in enumerate(skf.split(train_df['id'], train_df['has_cactus'], groups=train_df['sha1'])):",
      "    print(f\"===== Fold {fold+1}/{N_FOLDS} =====\")",
      "    tr_df = train_df.iloc[tr_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    va_df = train_df.iloc[va_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    print(f\"train size: {len(tr_df)} | valid size: {len(va_df)}\")",
      "",
      "    tr_ds = CactusDataset('train', tr_df, mode='train')",
      "    va_ds = CactusDataset('train', va_df, mode='valid')",
      "    tr_loader = DataLoader(tr_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True, drop_last=True)",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "",
      "    model = build_model().to(DEVICE)",
      "    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)",
      "    loss_fn = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss()",
      "    scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=(DEVICE=='cuda'))",
      "",
      "    best_auc = -1.0; best_state = None; no_improve = 0",
      "    t_fold0 = time.time()",
      "    for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS+1):",
      "        t0 = time.time()",
      "        tr_loss = train_one_epoch(model, tr_loader, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler)",
      "        val_auc, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "        elapsed = time.time()-t0",
      "        print(f\"[fold {fold}] epoch {epoch:02d} | tr_loss {tr_loss:.4f} | val_auc {val_auc:.6f} | {elapsed:.1f}s\", flush=True)",
      "        if val_auc > best_auc:",
      "            best_auc = val_auc; best_state = {k: v.cpu() for k,v in model.state_dict().items()}; no_improve = 0",
      "        else:",
      "            no_improve += 1",
      "        if no_improve >= 3:",
      "            print(f\"[fold {fold}] Early stopping at epoch {epoch}\")",
      "            break",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] best val AUC: {best_auc:.6f} | fold time {time.time()-t_fold0:.1f}s\")",
      "    all_fold_aucs.append(best_auc)",
      "    # Load best and infer OOF",
      "    model.load_state_dict({k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in best_state.items()}, strict=True)",
      "    # OOF preds with best",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    _, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "    oof[va_idx] = val_preds",
      "",
      "    # Test inference with simple TTA (orig + hflip)",
      "    test_ds = CactusDataset('test', test_ids, mode='test', tta_flip=None)",
      "    test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    p1, ids = predict_loader(model, test_loader)",
      "    test_ds_h = CactusDataset('test', test_ids, mode='test', tta_flip='hflip')",
      "    test_loader_h = DataLoader(test_ds_h, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "    p2, _ = predict_loader(model, test_loader_h)",
      "    p = (p1 + p2) / 2.0",
      "    test_pred_accum += p.astype(np.float32)",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] test inference done.\")",
      "    del model, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler, tr_loader, va_loader, test_loader, test_loader_h",
      "    gc.collect(); torch.cuda.empty_cache()",
      "",
      "print(f\"OOF AUC: {roc_auc_score(train_df['has_cactus'].values, oof):.6f}\")",
      "print(\"Fold AUCs:\", [f\"{a:.6f}\" for a in all_fold_aucs])",
      "",
      "# Save OOF",
      "np.save('oof_preds.npy', oof)",
      "pd.DataFrame({'id': train_df['id'], 'oof_pred': oof, 'has_cactus': train_df['has_cactus']}).to_csv('oof_preds.csv', index=False)",
      "",
      "# Average across folds",
      "test_pred = test_pred_accum / N_FOLDS",
      "",
      "# Exact-hash lookup (expected 0 overrides here)",
      "with open('train_sha1_label_map.json', 'r') as f:",
      "    sha1_to_label = json.load(f)",
      "test_hash_df = pd.read_csv('test_hashes.csv')",
      "lookup = test_hash_df[['id','sha1']].copy()",
      "lookup['label_from_train'] = lookup['sha1'].map(sha1_to_label).astype('float32')",
      "overrides = lookup['label_from_train'].notna().sum()",
      "print(f\"[inference] exact-hash overrides in test: {overrides}\")",
      "",
      "sub = test_ids.copy()",
      "sub = sub.merge(lookup[['id','label_from_train']], on='id', how='left')",
      "sub['pred'] = test_pred",
      "sub['has_cactus'] = np.where(sub['label_from_train'].notna(), sub['label_from_train'], sub['pred'])",
      "submission = sub[['id','has_cactus']].copy()",
      "submission.to_csv('submission.csv', index=False)",
      "print('Saved submission.csv with shape', submission.shape)"
    ],
    "cell_type": "code"
  },
  "explanation": "Fix validation target bug: validation dataset returned ids instead of labels, causing y to be a list. Added mode argument to CactusDataset to control labels vs transforms, and updated loaders accordingly. Next: execute cell 3 to continue training and produce submission."

      "N_FOLDS = 5",

      "WORKERS = 2",
      "LR_BACKBONE = 1e-4",
      "LR_HEAD = 1e-3",
      "WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4",
      "DEVICE = 'cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'",
      "",
      "def set_seed(seed=SEED):",
      "    random.seed(seed); np.random.seed(seed); torch.manual_seed(seed); torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)",
      "    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True",
      "    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False",
      "",

      "",
      "IMAGENET_MEAN = (0.485, 0.456, 0.406)",
      "IMAGENET_STD = (0.229, 0.224, 0.225)",
      "",
      "def get_transforms(train=True):",
      "    if train:",
      "        return A.Compose([",
      "            A.Resize(IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE, interpolation=3),",
      "            A.HorizontalFlip(p=0.5),",
      "            A.VerticalFlip(p=0.5),",
      "            A.ShiftScaleRotate(shift_limit=0.08, scale_limit=0.10, rotate_limit=10, border_mode=0, p=0.5),",
      "            A.ColorJitter(brightness=0.1, contrast=0.1, saturation=0.05, hue=0.0, p=0.2),",
      "            A.Normalize(mean=IMAGENET_MEAN, std=IMAGENET_STD),",
      "        ])",
      "    else:",
      "        return A.Compose([",
      "            A.Resize(IMG_SIZE, IMG_SIZE, interpolation=3),",
      "            A.Normalize(mean=IMAGENET_MEAN, std=IMAGENET_STD),",
      "        ])",
      "",
      "class CactusDataset(Dataset):",
      "    def __init__(self, img_dir, df, mode='train', tta_flip=None, tta_vflip=False):",
      "        # mode in {'train','valid','test'}",
      "        self.img_dir = Path(img_dir)",
      "        self.df = df.reset_index(drop=True)",
      "        self.mode = mode",
      "        self.tta_flip = tta_flip  # None or 'hflip'",
      "        self.tta_vflip = tta_vflip",
      "        self.tfms = get_transforms(train=(mode=='train'))",
      "    def __len__(self):",
      "        return len(self.df)",
      "    def __getitem__(self, idx):",
      "        row = self.df.iloc[idx]",
      "        img_path = self.img_dir / row['id']",
      "        with Image.open(img_path) as im:",
      "            im = im.convert('RGB')",
      "            img = np.array(im)",
      "        if self.tta_flip == 'hflip':",
      "            img = np.ascontiguousarray(img[:, ::-1, :])",
      "        if self.tta_vflip:",
      "            img = np.ascontiguousarray(img[::-1, :, :])",
      "        aug = self.tfms(image=img)",
      "        img_t = torch.from_numpy(aug['image'].transpose(2,0,1)).float()",
      "        if self.mode in ('train','valid'):",
      "            y = torch.tensor(row['has_cactus'], dtype=torch.float32)",
      "            return img_t, y",
      "        return img_t, row['id']",
      "",
      "def build_model():",
      "    model = models.resnet18(weights=ResNet18_Weights.IMAGENET1K_V1)",
      "    in_features = model.fc.in_features",
      "    model.fc = nn.Linear(in_features, 1)",
      "    return model",
      "",
      "def make_optimizer(model):",
      "    # Differential LR: smaller for backbone, larger for head",
      "    head_params = list(model.fc.parameters())",
      "    backbone_params = [p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if not n.startswith('fc.')]",
      "    return torch.optim.AdamW([",
      "        {'params': backbone_params, 'lr': LR_BACKBONE},",
      "        {'params': head_params, 'lr': LR_HEAD},",
      "    ], weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY)",
      "",
      "def train_one_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler):",
      "    model.train()",
      "    running = 0.0",
      "    for i, (x, y) in enumerate(loader):",
      "        x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True); y = y.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True).view(-1,1)",
      "        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)",
      "        with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', enabled=(DEVICE=='cuda')):",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            loss = loss_fn(logits, y)",
      "        scaler.scale(loss).backward()",
      "        scaler.step(optimizer); scaler.update()",
      "        running += loss.item()*x.size(0)",
      "        if (i+1) % 20 == 0:",
      "            print(f\"  [train] step {i+1}/{len(loader)} loss={running/((i+1)*loader.batch_size):.4f}\", flush=True)",
      "    return running/len(loader.dataset)",
      "",
      "def valid_one_epoch(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    preds, targs = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, y in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True); y = y.view(-1,1)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            targs.append(y.squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "    preds = np.concatenate(preds); targs = np.concatenate(targs)",
      "    auc = roc_auc_score(targs, preds)",
      "    return auc, preds",
      "",
      "def predict_loader(model, loader):",
      "    model.eval()",
      "    out_ids, out_preds = [], []",
      "    with torch.no_grad():",
      "        for x, ids in loader:",
      "            x = x.to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)",
      "            logits = model(x)",
      "            out_preds.append(torch.sigmoid(logits).squeeze(1).cpu().numpy())",
      "            out_ids += list(ids)",
      "    return np.concatenate(out_preds), out_ids",
      "",
      "# Load data and groups",
      "train_df = pd.read_csv('train.csv')",
      "groups_df = pd.read_csv('cv_groups.csv')",
      "train_df = train_df.merge(groups_df[['id','sha1']], on='id', how='left')",
      "test_ids = pd.read_csv('sample_submission.csv')",
      "",
      "skf = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)",
      "oof = np.zeros(len(train_df), dtype=np.float32)",
      "test_pred_accum = np.zeros(len(test_ids), dtype=np.float32)",
      "",
      "all_fold_aucs = []",
      "t_all = time.time()",
      "for fold, (tr_idx, va_idx) in enumerate(skf.split(train_df['id'], train_df['has_cactus'], groups=train_df['sha1'])):",
      "    print(f\"===== Fold {fold+1}/{N_FOLDS} =====\")",
      "    tr_df = train_df.iloc[tr_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    va_df = train_df.iloc[va_idx].reset_index(drop=True)",
      "    print(f\"train size: {len(tr_df)} | valid size: {len(va_df)}\")",
      "",
      "    tr_ds = CactusDataset('train', tr_df, mode='train')",
      "    va_ds = CactusDataset('train', va_df, mode='valid')",
      "    tr_loader = DataLoader(tr_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True, drop_last=True)",
      "    va_loader = DataLoader(va_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=WORKERS, pin_memory=True)",
      "",
      "    model = build_model().to(DEVICE)",
      "    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)",
      "    scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=EPOCHS, eta_min=1e-5)",
      "    loss_fn = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss()",
      "    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler('cuda', enabled=(DEVICE=='cuda'))",
      "",
      "    best_auc = -1.0; best_state = None; no_improve = 0",
      "    t_fold0 = time.time()",
      "    for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS+1):",
      "        t0 = time.time()",
      "        tr_loss = train_one_epoch(model, tr_loader, optimizer, loss_fn, scaler)",
      "        val_auc, val_preds = valid_one_epoch(model, va_loader)",
      "        scheduler.step()",
      "        elapsed = time.time()-t0",
      "        # Log current LRs",
      "        lrs = [pg['lr'] for pg in optimizer.param_groups]",
      "        print(f\"[fold {fold}] epoch {epoch:02d} | tr_loss {tr_loss:.4f} | val_auc {val_auc:.6f} | lrs {lrs} | {elapsed:.1f}s\", flush=True)",
      "        if val_auc > best_auc:",
      "            best_auc = val_auc; best_state = {k: v.cpu() for k,v in model.state_dict().items()}; no_improve = 0",
      "        else:",
      "            no_improve += 1",
      "        if no_improve >= 3:",
      "            print(f\"[fold {fold}] Early stopping at epoch {epoch}\")",
      "            break",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] best val AUC: {best_auc:.6f} | fold time {time.time()-t_fold0:.1f}s\")",
      "    all_fold_aucs.append(best_auc)",
      "    # Load best and infer OOF",
      "    model.load_state_dict({k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in best_state.items()}, strict=True)",

EXPERT ADVICE: Prioritized path to 1.00000 LB (fastest first)

1) Re-infer current model with Dihedral-8 TTA and logit averaging (no retrain)
- Views: rot90 k in {0,1,2,3} × {no flip, hflip} = 8.
- Ensemble rule: average raw logits across TTAs and folds, then sigmoid once.
- Expected runtime (T4): ~2–3x your current test inference per fold; total a couple of minutes.
- Submit. If <1.00000, go to 2.

2) Add 2 more seeds at 96px; EMA on; logit-ensemble (fast, most reliable)
- Seeds: keep 42; add 2025 and 777 (or 43/44).
- Config: identical to current (end-to-end, diff LRs), EPOCHS=12, patience=3, AMP on.
- EMA: decay=0.999; validate/infer with EMA weights.
- Ensembling:
  - Per fold: collect logits.
  - TTA: use the same Dihedral-8 at inference; average logits over TTAs and folds.
  - Across seeds: average logits across seeds; sigmoid once at end.
- Early interrupt: by epoch 3, if fold val AUC < 0.99990, stop that fold; if ≥2 folds in a seed miss, abort that seed.
- Expected runtime (T4): ~7–9 min per 5-fold seed; two seeds ~15–18 min.
- Submit. If <1.00000, go to 3.

3) One quick 128px run; blend with 96px ensemble
- IMG_SIZE=128, batch_size≈128, EPOCHS=8–10, patience=2–3, same augs/LRs, end-to-end.
- TTA: Dihedral-8; logit-avg with existing 96px seeds.
- Early interrupt: if by epoch 3 fold val AUC doesn’t exceed your 96px fold best by >1e-5, abort.
- Expected runtime (T4): ~12–15 min for one 5-fold seed.
- Submit. If <1.00000, go to 4.

4) Refine CV grouping with phash (only if still sticky), then 1 seed
- Cluster near-dups by phash Hamming distance ≤4–5 (union-find), use cluster_id as groups in StratifiedGroupKFold.
- Run 1 seed at 96px or 128px; blend logits with your best ensemble.
- Cost: clustering <1–2 min; training as usual.

What to skip or deprioritize
- MixUp/CutMix, heavy augs: unnecessary on this tiny/clean binary task; can hurt.
- Label smoothing: usually no benefit here; skip for speed/simplicity.
- 224px: slower with minimal gain—use 128px if you upscale.
- Complex post-proc: stick to logit averaging; rank-avg only as last resort if combining very heterogeneous models.

Post-processing rules for AUC
- Always ensemble logits across folds, TTAs, and seeds; apply sigmoid once.
- Keep probability averaging out; it can subtly change ranks and hurt marginal gains. Rank-avg only if mixing very different backbones/resolutions and logit-avg underperforms.

Concrete execution (under ~30 minutes total)
- Step 1 now: run Dihedral-8 TTA with logit averaging on your existing 5-fold model; submit.
- If needed: train seeds 2025 and 777 at 96px with EMA; infer with Dihedral-8 TTA; logit-avg over folds and seeds; submit.
- If still needed: one 128px seed; logit-blend with 96px ensemble; submit.

Priority actions (do now)
- phash-based test overrides (Grok, Claude; highest impact)
  - Convert 16-char hex phash to 64-bit vectors; compute Hamming distance train↔test.
  - For each test image, if nearest train phash distance ≤2–4 (tune), set test prob = train label (majority if multiple close matches). Else use model prob.
  - If multiple close train matches with mixed labels, average labels or pick majority; back off to model if tie.
  - Add a simple distance histogram plot and tune threshold to maximize OOF/holdout performance.
- phash grouping for CV (Grok, OpenAI)
  - Cluster near-duplicates by phash (e.g., DBSCAN with Hamming metric; or group by nearest neighbor ≤2) and use group IDs in StratifiedGroupKFold to stabilize OOF and prevent leakage.
- Stronger inference ensembling (OpenAI)
  - D4 TTA: 8-way (rot 0/90/180/270 × hflip on/off). Optionally multi-scale [64, 96, 128] and average.
  - Train 3–5 seeds of the same ResNet18 setup; average fold×seed predictions.
  - Add a second lightweight backbone (resnet34 or efficientnet_b0 from torchvision) and blend.

Training tweaks (small, safe gains)
- Longer training with patience 5–7; consider 15–20 epochs (Grok, OpenAI).
- Weight EMA or SWA; evaluate with EMA/SWA weights (OpenAI, Grok).
- Mildly stronger augs: Mixup/Cutout; keep augs light if input upscaled (Grok, OpenAI).
- Class imbalance: weighted BCE (higher weight for class 0) (Grok).
- Optionally increase input to 128 with anti-aliased resize (Grok, OpenAI).

Data/label hygiene
- Inspect consistent OOF errors; drop or relabel obvious mislabels if found (Claude, Grok).
- Pseudo-label high-confidence test images (especially phash matches) and retrain (Grok).

De-prioritized/optional
- Switching to tiny custom CNNs or staying at native 32×32 is lower priority given current ResNet18 success; keep 96–128 with pretrained backbones (OpenAI > Claude).
- Larger backbones (ResNet50/EfficientNet) can be tried if time allows (Grok).

Pitfalls to avoid
- Leakage via duplicate splits: must use phash groups in CV (all coaches).
- Misordered TTA/test IDs when averaging; ensure consistent ID alignment (OpenAI).
- Wrong loss/normalization or forgetting eval/no_grad (OpenAI).

Minimal implementation guide in your notebook
- Cell 2: After phash computation, build train phash bit arrays.
- New helper: phash_hex_to_bits -> np.uint8[64]; hamming(a,b). Precompute train bit matrix.
- Cell 3 (inference): For each test id, find nearest train by Hamming (brute-force OK at this scale). If dist ≤ threshold, override with train label; else keep ensembled TTA prob.
- CV: Replace sha1 groups with phash-cluster group IDs.

      "        else: no_imp+=1",
      "        if no_imp>=3: print(f\"[fold {fold}] early stop at {epoch}\"); break",
      "    print(f\"[fold {fold}] best val AUC: {best_auc:.6f} | fold time {time.time()-t_fold:.1f}s\")",
      "    fold_aucs.append(best_auc)",
